## Canada--Nova Scotia Offshore Area Petroleum Operations Framework Regulations {#canadanova-scotia-offshore-area-petroleum-operations-framework-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2024-26
:::

[CANADA-NOVA SCOTIA OFFSHORE PETROLEUM RESOURCES ACCORD IMPLEMENTATION
ACT](/eng/acts/C-7.8)

Registration 2024-02-19

Canada--Nova Scotia Offshore Area Petroleum Operations Framework
Regulations

P.C. 2024-144 2024-02-19
::::

:::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Whereas, under subsection 154(1) of the [Canada-Nova Scotia Offshore
Petroleum Resources Accord Implementation
Act](/eng/acts/C-7.8)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30039){#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30039-ID0EFBCA},
a copy of the proposed [Canada--Nova Scotia Offshore Area Petroleum
Operations Framework Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2024-26),
substantially in the annexed form, was published in the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/), Part I, on June 18, 2022 and a
reasonable opportunity was afforded to interested persons to make
representations to the Minister of Natural Resources with respect to the
proposed Regulations;

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30039-ID0EFBCA){#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30039}S.C.
  1988, c. 28
  :::

And whereas, under subsection 6(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30327){#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30327-ID0EBBBA}
of that Act, the Minister of Natural Resources consulted the Provincial
Minister for Nova Scotia with respect to the proposed Regulations and
that minister approved the making of those Regulations;

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30327-ID0EBBBA){#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30327}S.C.
  2015, c. 4, ss. 72 and 117(16)
  :::

Therefore, Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the
recommendation of the Minister of Natural Resources and the Minister of
the Environment, makes the annexed [Canada--Nova Scotia Offshore Area
Petroleum Operations Framework
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2024-26) under section 153[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30328){#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30328-ID0EEBAA}
of the [Canada-Nova Scotia Offshore Petroleum Resources Accord
Implementation Act](/eng/acts/C-7.8)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30039){#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30039-ID0EBBAA}.

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30328-ID0EEBAA){#fn_81000-2-4655_hq_30328}S.C.
  2015, c. 4, ss. 90 and 117(19)
  :::
:::

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1444592 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in these Regulations.

[[accidental event]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[accidental event]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an unexpected or
    unplanned event or circumstance or series of unexpected or unplanned
    events or circumstances that may lead to the loss of life or damage
    to the environment, including pollution. ([événement
    accidentel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[accommodations area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[accommodations area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the area of an
    installation or vessel that contains the sleeping quarters, dining
    areas, food preparation areas, general recreation areas, offices and
    medical rooms and includes all washrooms in that area. ([aire
    d'habitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[accommodations installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[accommodations installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    installation that is used to accommodate persons at a production
    site, drill site or dive site and that functions independently of a
    production installation, drilling installation or diving
    installation. ([installation d'habitation]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canada-Nova Scotia Offshore
    Petroleum Resources Accord Implementation
    Act](/eng/acts/C-7.8). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[authorization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[authorization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an authorization issued
    by the Board under paragraph 142(1)(b) of the
    Act. ([autorisation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[authorized inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[authorized inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is
    recognized under the laws of Canada or of a province as qualified to
    inspect boilers and pressure systems or a representative of a
    certifying authority who is qualified to carry out that
    function. ([inspecteur autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[barrier element]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[barrier element]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a physical element
    that on its own does not prevent the flow of fluids but that in
    combination with other physical elements forms a well
    barrier. ([élément de barrière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[barrier envelope]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[barrier envelope]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an envelope
    consisting of a set of barrier elements that prevents any unintended
    flow of fluids from the formation into the well-bore, another
    formation or the environment. ([enveloppe de
    barrière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[certificate of fitness]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[certificate of fitness]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a certificate
    referred to in section 143.2 of the Act. ([certificat
    d'aptitude]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[certifying authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[certifying authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the American
    Bureau of Shipping, Bureau Veritas, Det norske Veritas or Lloyd's
    Register. ([autorité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[classification society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[classification society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a member of the
    International Association of Classification Societies that has
    recognized and relevant competence and experience in, and
    established rules and procedures for, the classification of fixed
    and floating structures, including vessels, that are used in oil or
    gas activities in locations with physical and environmental
    conditions similar to those of the offshore area. ([société de
    classification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[commingled production]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[commingled production]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the production
    of petroleum from more than one pool or zone through a common well
    where the production from each pool or zone is not measured
    separately. ([production mélangée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[completion interval]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[completion interval]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a section within a
    well that is prepared to permit

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the production of fluids from the well;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the observation of the performance of the
      reservoir; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the injection of fluids into the
      well. ([intervalle de complétion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[control centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[control centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a continuously staffed
    work area in which a control system that is critical to the
    operation of an installation or a pipeline, to safety or to the
    prevention of waste and pollution is located. ([centre de
    commande]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[control system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[control system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any system, station or
    panel used to monitor the status and control the operation of
    equipment used for or in support of the drilling for, or the
    production, processing or transportation of, petroleum or any
    system, station or panel used to monitor and control the operation
    of an installation. ([système de commande]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[decommissioning and abandonment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[decommissioning and abandonment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    carrying out of the following processes in accordance with any
    applicable Act of Parliament, any applicable regulation made under
    an Act of Parliament, the applicable authorization and any approved
    development plans:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cessation of operations;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the controlled abandonment of all wells;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the retirement from service and abandonment or
      removal of all installations, including their systems and
      equipment; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the retirement from service and abandonment or
      removal of all pipelines and materials. ([désaffectation et
      abandon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[delineation well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[delineation well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 122(1) of the Act. ([puits de
    délimitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[development well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[development well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 122(1) of the Act. ([puits
    d'exploitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[diving installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[diving installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an installation or
    vessel on which a diving system is installed. ([installation de
    plongée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[diving project]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[diving project]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any work or activity
    that is related to the exploration or drilling for, or the
    production, conservation, processing or transportation of, petroleum
    and that involves diving. ([projet de plongée]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[diving system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[diving system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the equipment that is
    required to execute a dive, including that required for compression,
    decompression, rescue and recovery. ([système de
    plongée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[drilling installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[drilling installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a drilling unit
    or a drilling rig, and the stable foundation on which it is
    installed --- including an artificial island, an ice platform, a
    floating platform, a platform fixed to the seabed and any other
    foundation specifically used for drilling --- and any associated
    accommodations area. ([installation de forage]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[drilling program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[drilling program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a program for the
    drilling of one or more wells within a specified time and within
    specified areas through the use of one or more drilling
    installations and includes any work or activity related to the
    program. ([programme de forage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[drilling rig]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[drilling rig]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the equipment used to
    conduct well operations and associated systems, including power
    systems, control systems and monitoring systems. ([appareil de
    forage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[drilling riser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[drilling riser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the connection between
    a subsea blowout preventer and a mobile offshore platform. ([tube
    prolongateur de forage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[drilling unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[drilling unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fixed or mobile
    offshore platform, or a vessel used in any well operation, that is
    fitted with a drilling rig, including all systems and equipment
    installed on the platform or vessel that are related to well
    operations and marine activities. ([unité de
    forage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[drill site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[drill site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a location where a drilling
    rig is or is proposed to be installed. ([emplacement de
    forage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[environmental load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[environmental load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a load imposed by
    meteorological or oceanographic conditions, such as winds, waves,
    tides, currents or snow, ice conditions, regional ice features, such
    as sea ice or icebergs, a seismic event or any other naturally
    occurring phenomenon. ([charge environnementale]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[environmental program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[environmental program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a program
    pertaining to an [[environmental study]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as
    defined in subsection 122(1) of the Act. ([programme
    environnemental]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[exploratory well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[exploratory well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 122(1) of the Act. ([puits
    d'exploration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[floating platform]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[floating platform]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a column-stabilized
    mobile offshore platform, a surface mobile offshore platform or a
    fixed floating offshore platform, including a tension leg platform
    or a spar platform. ([plate-forme flottante]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[flow allocation procedure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[flow allocation procedure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    procedure

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to allocate total measured quantities of
      petroleum and water produced from or injected into a pool or zone
      back to individual wells in a pool or zone where individual well
      production or injection is not measured separately; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to allocate production among fields whose
      petroleum is combined for the purpose of storage or
      processing. ([méthode de répartition du débit]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[flow calculation procedure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[flow calculation procedure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    procedure to convert raw meter output to a measured quantity of
    petroleum or water. ([méthode de calcul du débit]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[flowline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[flowline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any line, other than a
    pipeline, that is used to transport fluids between a well and
    equipment used for the production of petroleum that is located at a
    production site or to transport fluids between a well and any
    systems or equipment that are used in support of that production and
    between those systems or equipment and the production
    equipment. ([conduite d'écoulement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[flow system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[flow system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the flow meters, auxiliary
    equipment attached to the flow meters, fluid sampling devices,
    production test equipment, master meter and meter prover used to
    measure and record the rate and volumes at which fluids are

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} produced from or injected into a pool;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} used as a fuel;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} used for artificial lift; or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} flared, vented or transferred from a production
      installation. ([système d'écoulement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[formation flow test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[formation flow test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an operation

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to induce the flow of formation fluids to procure
      reservoir fluid samples and determine reservoir flow
      characteristics; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to inject fluids into a formation to evaluate
      injectivity. ([essai d'écoulement de formation]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[functional load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[functional load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any construction load
    or operating load other than an environmental load or accidental
    load. ([charge fonctionnelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[geoscientific program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[geoscientific program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any program that
    involves [[geological work]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or [[geophysical
    work]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as those terms are defined in
    subsection 122(1) of the Act. ([programme
    géoscientifique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[geotechnical program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[geotechnical program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any program that
    involves [[geotechnical work]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in
    subsection 122(1) of the Act, that is undertaken to assess whether
    the seabed or shallow subsurface, as the case may be, is suitable to
    support installations or any other structures. ([programme
    géotechnique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in Part 5, a
    drilling installation, production installation or accommodations
    installation. ([installation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[life-saving appliances]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[life-saving appliances]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes lifebuoys,
    survival craft, launching and embarkation appliances, marine
    evacuation systems and visual signals. ([engins de
    sauvetage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a functional load,
    environmental load, accidental load and abnormal
    load. ([charge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[LSA Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[LSA Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the annex to International
    Maritime Organization Resolution MSC.48(66), International
    Life-Saving Appliance (LSA) Code. ([recueil LSA]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[major accidental event]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[major accidental event]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an unexpected
    or unplanned event or circumstance or series of unexpected or
    unplanned events or circumstances that may lead to the loss of more
    than one life or uncontrolled pollution. ([événement accidentel
    majeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[marine activities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[marine activities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means activities related
    to the stability, station-keeping and collision avoidance of
    floating platforms and includes mooring, dynamic positioning and
    ballasting. ([activités maritimes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[mobile offshore platform]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mobile offshore platform]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a platform
    that is designed to operate in a floating or buoyant mode or that
    can be moved from place to place without major dismantling or
    modification, whether or not it has its own motive
    power. ([plate-forme mobile extracôtière]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[operations site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[operations site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a site where an
    authorized work or activity is carried out. ([emplacement des
    opérations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that holds an
    operating licence issued by the Board under paragraph 142(1)(a) of
    the Act and applies for or has been granted an
    authorization. ([exploitant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[physical and environmental conditions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[physical and environmental conditions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    the physical, geotechnical, seismic, oceanographic, meteorological
    or ice conditions that might affect an authorized work or
    activity. ([conditions physiques et
    environnementales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pipeline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pipeline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in CSA
    Group standard Z662, Oil and gas pipeline systems, as it relates to
    offshore pipelines. ([pipeline]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pollution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pollution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the introduction into the
    environment of any substance or form of energy outside the limits
    applicable to an authorized work or
    activity. ([pollution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pressure system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pressure system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means piping, pressure
    vessels, safety components and pressure components, including
    elements attached to pressurized parts, such as flanges, nozzles,
    couplings, supports, lifting lugs, safety valves and
    gauges. ([système sous pression]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[production installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[production installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the systems and equipment used for or in support
      of the production of petroleum, including those that are used for
      separation, treatment and processing;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the systems and equipment used to conduct well
      operations;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any systems and equipment related to marine
      activities;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any associated aircraft landing areas, storage
      areas or tanks and accommodations areas; and

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any associated platforms, artificial islands,
      subsea production systems and offshore loading systems. ([ouvrage
      de production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[production project]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[production project]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a project for the
    purpose of developing a production site on, or producing petroleum
    from, a pool or field, including any work or activity related to the
    project. ([projet de production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[production riser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[production riser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the connection
    between subsea production equipment and a production
    platform. ([tube prolongateur de production]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[production site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[production site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a site where a
    production installation is or is proposed to be
    installed. ([emplacement de production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[recovery of petroleum]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[recovery of petroleum]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the recovery of
    petroleum under foreseeable economic and operational
    conditions. ([récupération des hydrocarbures]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[relief well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[relief well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a well that is drilled to
    assist in controlling a blowout in an existing well. ([puits de
    secours]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[reportable incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[reportable incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an event that
    resulted in any of the following occurrences or in which an
    occurrence referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to (f) was narrowly
    avoided:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} loss of life;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fire or explosion;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} collision;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} pollution;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} leak of a hazardous substance;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} loss of well control;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} impairment of a support craft or of any of the
      structural elements of an installation --- or any system or
      equipment --- that is critical to safety;

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} impairment of any of the structural elements of
      an installation --- or any system or equipment --- critical to
      environmental protection;

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} implementation of emergency response
      procedures. ([incident à signaler]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[safety-critical element]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[safety-critical element]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any system or
    equipment, including software and temporary or portable equipment,
    that is critical to the safety or integrity of an installation or to
    preventing the installation from polluting, including

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any system or equipment

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is intended to prevent or limit the
        effects of a hazard that could cause a major accidental event,
        or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} whose failure could

        - [(A)]{.lawlabel} cause a hazard that could cause a major
          accidental event, or

        - [(B)]{.lawlabel} worsen the effects on the installation of a
          major accidental event; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any software or temporary or portable equipment
      that affects any system or equipment referred to in paragraph
      (a). ([élément essentiel à la sécurité]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[subsea production system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[subsea production system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means equipment and
    structures that are located on or below the seabed for the
    production of petroleum from, or for the injection of fluids into, a
    field under a production site and includes production risers,
    flowlines and associated control systems that are located upstream
    of the isolation valve. ([système de production
    sous-marin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[support craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[support craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel, vehicle,
    aircraft or other craft used to provide transportation or assistance
    to persons at an operations site. ([véhicule de
    service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[waste material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[waste material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any garbage, refuse,
    sewage or waste fluids or any other useless material that is
    generated during the carrying out of any work or activity, including
    used or surplus drill cuttings and drilling fluid as well as
    produced water. ([déchets]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[watertight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[watertight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means designed and constructed to
    withstand a static head of water without any
    leakage. ([étanche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[well control]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[well control]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the control of the
    movement of fluids into or from a well. ([maîtrise du
    puits]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[well operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[well operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an operation related to
    the drilling, completion, recompletion, re-entry, intervention,
    workover, suspension or abandonment of a well. ([travaux relatifs au
    puits]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[workover]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[workover]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an operation on a completed
    well that requires removal of the tree or the
    tubing. ([reconditionnement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any stratum or any sequence of
    strata, including a zone that has been designated as such by the
    Board under paragraph 60(a). ([couche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In these
  Regulations, any incorporation by reference of a document is an
  incorporation of that document as amended from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bilingual documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if a document that is
  incorporated by reference is available in both official languages, any
  amendment to it is incorporated only when the amended version is
  available in both official languages.

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Experience, Training, Qualifications and Competence]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1444703 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must ensure that any person to whom a duty is assigned or who
  carries out a work or activity under these Regulations has the
  necessary experience, training, qualifications and competence to carry
  out that duty, work or activity safely, in a manner that protects the
  environment and in compliance with these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sufficient number and supervision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator must ensure that the persons referred to
  in subsection (1) are sufficient in number and receive the necessary
  supervision to ensure safety and the protection of the environment.

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Management System]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1444709 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must, for the purposes of reducing safety and environmental
  risks, preventing pollution and ensuring the conservation of petroleum
  resources, develop a management system that meets the following
  requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it must be set out in writing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must apply to all of the works and activities
    referred to in the operator's application for authorization;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must correspond to the scope, nature and
    complexity of the works and activities and the associated hazards
    and risks;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it must be explicit, comprehensive and proactive;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} it must foster a culture of safety;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} it must establish conditions under which a person
    who makes a report that relates to safety or protection of the
    environment will be protected from reprisal;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for integrating works and
    activities and technical systems with the management of human and
    financial resources;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes to ensure that all
    persons have the necessary experience, training, qualifications and
    competence and receive the necessary supervision to carry out the
    duties they are assigned;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it must set out the roles, responsibilities and
    authorities of all persons exercising functions under it, as well as
    the processes for making those persons aware of their roles,
    responsibilities and authorities;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for coordinating the
    carrying out and management of the works and activities among the
    operator, employers, suppliers, service providers and other persons
    that are subject to it;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for the internal and
    external communication of documents and information relating to
    safety or the protection of the environment;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} it must include a process for the efficient and
    immediate transmission, at every shift handover, of documents and
    information relating to any conditions, mechanical or procedural
    deficiencies or other problems that may have an impact on safety or
    the protection of the environment;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for identifying hazards that may arise during
      routine and non-routine operations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for assessing the risks associated with those
      hazards and for reducing those risks through the implementation of
      control measures, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for establishing an inventory of those hazards
      and control measures and for maintaining that inventory;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for investigating and
    reporting, for the purposes of section 179, the root causes of any
    reportable incident, the contributing factors and the measures to be
    implemented to prevent recurrence of the incident;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} it must include a process for establishing a system
    to analyze trends in hazards and reportable incidents;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for identifying,
    evaluating and managing all systems and equipment that are critical
    to safety or to the protection of the environment;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for identifying,
    evaluating and managing any changes that could affect safety, the
    protection of the environment and the conservation of petroleum
    resources;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for identifying tasks
    that are critical to safety, the protection of the environment and
    the conservation of petroleum resources;

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for establishing and
    maintaining measurable goals and performance indicators that are
    applicable to it;

  - [(t)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for its periodic internal
    audit and review to identify areas for improvement and the
    preventive and corrective measures to be taken if deficiencies are
    identified;

  - [(u)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for monitoring compliance
    and preventing non-compliance with the requirements of these
    Regulations, the provisions of Part III of the Act and any
    requirements that are determined by the Board under that Part;

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for inspection,
    monitoring, testing and maintenance to ensure the continued
    integrity of all installations, including their systems and
    equipment, pipelines and vessels, and for the taking of corrective
    measures if deficiencies are identified;

  - [(w)]{.lawlabel} it must include the policies and indicate the
    standards on which it is based;

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} it must include a process to ensure that all
    documents associated with it are approved by a person with the
    necessary authority, periodically reviewed and updated when
    necessary;

  - [(y)]{.lawlabel} it must include a process for establishing a system
    to manage any records associated with it, and the records necessary
    to support operational and regulatory requirements, for the purpose
    of ensuring that those records are generated, identified, controlled
    and retained and are readily accessible for consultation and
    examination; and

  - [(z)]{.lawlabel} it must include processes for controlling and
    coordinating work, including with respect to the issuance of work
    permits required under Part 10 and the identification of the works
    and activities for which a work permit is required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documentation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator must ensure that the processes and
  policies included in the management system and the standards referred
  to in it are readily accessible for consultation and examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Organization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The documentation associated with the management
  system must be organized and set out in a logical fashion to allow for
  ease of understanding and efficient implementation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Processes and procedures

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, a reference to a process includes
  any procedures that are necessary to implement the process.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Human resources

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must put in place an organizational structure that includes
  sufficient human resources to implement and continually improve the
  management system.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accountable person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator must designate an employee as the
  accountable person for the management system and must ensure that the
  accountable person has the necessary authority over the human and
  financial resources that are required to implement and continually
  improve the system.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Name, position and contact information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator must ensure that the name, position and
  contact information of the accountable person is submitted to the
  Board at the time the application for an authorization is made, when a
  new designation is made under subsection (2) and any time a change is
  made to the name, position or contact information of the accountable
  person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Implementation

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must ensure that the management system is implemented before
  the commencement of any authorized work or activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator must ensure that all employees,
  employers, suppliers, service providers and other persons that are
  subject to the management system comply with the requirements of the
  management system.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continual improvement

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** The accountable person
referred to in subsection 5(2) must ensure that the management system is
continually improved.

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Authorization]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1444762 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1444763 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents and information

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** The application for an
authorization must be accompanied by the following documents and
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the scope of the proposed work or activity;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an execution plan and schedule for undertaking the
  proposed work or activity;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the safety plan referred to in section 9;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the environmental protection plan referred to in
  section 10;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the contingency plan referred to in section 11;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of the installations, including their
  systems and equipment, pipelines, vessels and support craft, that are
  to be used for carrying out the work or activity, including the
  layouts of the installations;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a production project, a description of
  the field data acquisition program referred to in section 13;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a drilling program or a production
  project,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} information on

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} any proposed flaring or venting of gas, including
      the rationale for flaring or venting and the estimated rate,
      quantity and period of the flaring or venting, and

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} any proposed burning of oil, including the
      rationale for burning and the estimated quantity of oil to be
      burned, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the decommissioning and abandonment plan referred
    to in section 15;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a geoscientific program, geotechnical
  program or environmental program,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a map illustrating the location of the program
    works and activities and their proximity to any man-made structures
    or vulnerable natural structures, as well as any territorial or
    other boundaries,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the methods to be used in
    carrying out the program works and activities and a description of
    any aircraft or vessel to be used, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the proposed data acquisition
    plan;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a diving project, the dive project
  plan required under section 171 of the [Canada--Nova Scotia Offshore
  Area Occupational Health and Safety
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2021-248); and

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the list required under paragraph
  151(a), the records made in the course of conducting the risk
  assessment required under paragraph 151(b) and the action plan
  required under paragraph 151(c).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safety plan

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must develop a safety plan that sets out the procedures,
  practices, resources, sequence of key safety-related activities and
  monitoring measures that are necessary to safely carry out a proposed
  work or activity, as well as the target levels of safety in respect of
  the work or activity and measures for hazard management.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents and information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The safety plan must include the following documents
  and information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specific references to and detailed descriptions of
    the provisions of the management system that relate to safety,
    sufficient to demonstrate how the obligations set out in these
    Regulations with regard to safety will be fulfilled;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document that includes

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a summary of the studies that have been carried
      out, and a description of the processes that will be followed, for
      the purposes of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} identifying hazards related to the proposed
        work or activity that may occur during routine and non-routine
        operations, including any hazards posed by any other activities
        taking place near the proposed work or activity, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} assessing safety risks associated with the
        identified hazards,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the identified hazards referred
      to in clause (i)(A) and the results of the assessments referred to
      in clause (i)(B),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a summary of the measures to be implemented to
      anticipate safety risks related to the identified hazards,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a summary and evaluation of the measures to be
      implemented to reduce the safety risks associated with the
      identified hazards, including, if the possibility of ice hazards
      exists, measures for ice detection, forecasting, surveillance and
      reporting, including data collection, and any measures for ice
      avoidance or deflection,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of the measures to be
      implemented to reduce safety risks to a level that is as low as
      reasonably practicable in respect of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the design of all installations, including
        their systems and equipment,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the design, winterization and operation of any
        installation that is to be operated in a cold climate,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the design, arrangement, installation and
        maintenance of barriers to provide fire and blast protection,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the design of all control systems,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the design, selection, location, installation,
        commissioning, protection, operation, inspection and maintenance
        of mechanical equipment,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} the design, construction, installation,
        commissioning, operation, inspection, monitoring, testing and
        maintenance of any subsea production system under all
        foreseeable physical and environmental conditions and operating
        conditions for all modes of operation,

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} the management of temporary or portable
        equipment, and

      - [(H)]{.lawlabel} the arrangement and specification of watertight
        and weathertight appliances,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of the measures to be
      implemented in respect of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the design and location of any vent that is
        used to release gas into the atmosphere without combustion in
        order to minimize the risk of accidental ignition of the gas,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the design, selection, operation, inspection,
        testing and maintenance of fire protection systems and equipment
        in order to minimize the risk of hazards to persons who use
        those systems and equipment,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the design of boilers and pressure systems in
        order to minimize the risk of hazards to the installation and to
        persons present on it and to any other installations, vessel or
        persons in proximity to it, and

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the design and maintenance of any
        disconnectable mooring system on a floating platform to ensure
        that the risk that the system will fail to safely disconnect if
        exposed to situations that would exceed the platform's
        structural limits or the system's design limits is reduced to a
        level that is as low as reasonably practicable, without
        compromising the ability to achieve the target levels of safety
        set out in the safety plan and environmental protection plan,
        and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a summary of the measures to be implemented for
      communicating the identified hazards and for mitigating the safety
      risks associated with those hazards to all persons who are
      directly affected;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of all installations or vessels that
    are to be used during the proposed work or activity, a description
    of their systems and equipment that are critical to safety and a
    brief description of the systems in place for the inspection,
    testing and maintenance of those systems and that equipment;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the organizational structure and
    chain of command for the proposed work or activity that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} explains the relationship between the
      organizational structure and chain of command, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} includes the name, position and contact
      information of the employee who is responsible for the management
      of the safety plan; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures to be implemented to
    monitor compliance with the plan and to evaluate performance in
    relation to its objectives.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Environmental protection plan

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must develop an environmental protection plan that sets out
  the procedures, practices, resources and monitoring measures that are
  necessary to protect the environment from the effects of a proposed
  work or activity, the target levels of safety in respect of the work
  or activity and measures for hazard management.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents and information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The environmental protection plan must include the
  following documents and information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specific references to and detailed descriptions of
    the provisions of the management system that relate to the
    protection of the environment, sufficient to demonstrate how the
    obligations set out in these Regulations with regard to
    environmental protection will be fulfilled;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document that includes

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a summary of the studies that have been carried
      out, and a description of the processes that will be followed, for
      the purposes of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} identifying hazards related to the proposed
        work or activity that may occur during routine and non-routine
        operations, including any hazards posed by any other activities
        taking place near the proposed work or activity, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} assessing environmental risks associated with
        the identified hazards,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the identified hazards referred
      to in clause (i)(A) and the results of the assessments referred to
      in clause (i)(B),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a summary of the measures to be implemented to
      anticipate environmental risks related to the identified hazards,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a summary and evaluation of the measures to be
      implemented to reduce the environmental risks associated with the
      identified hazards, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of the measures to be
      implemented to reduce environmental risks to a level that is as
      low as reasonably practicable in respect of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the design of all installations, including
        their systems and equipment,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the design, winterization and operation of any
        installation that is to be operated in a cold climate,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the design, arrangement, installation and
        maintenance of barriers to provide fire and blast protection,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the design of all control systems,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the design, selection, location, installation,
        commissioning, protection, operation, inspection and maintenance
        of mechanical equipment,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} the design, construction, installation,
        commissioning, operation, inspection, monitoring, testing and
        maintenance of any subsea production system under all
        foreseeable physical and environmental conditions and operating
        conditions for all modes of operation, and

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} the management of temporary or portable
        equipment, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of the measures to be
      implemented in respect of the design and location of any vent that
      is used to release gas into the atmosphere without combustion in
      order to minimize the risk of accidental ignition of the gas, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a summary of the measures to be implemented for
      communicating the identified hazards and for mitigating the
      environmental risks associated with those hazards to all persons
      who are directly affected;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of all installations or vessels that
    are to be used during the proposed work or activity, a description
    of their systems and equipment that are critical to the protection
    of the environment and a brief description of the systems in place
    for the inspection, testing and maintenance of those systems and
    that equipment;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a drilling program or a production
    project, the procedures for the selection, evaluation and use of
    chemical substances, including process chemicals and drilling fluid
    ingredients;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the equipment and procedures for
    the treatment, handling and disposal of waste material;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of all of the discharge streams and
    the limits of any discharge into the environment, including any
    discharge of waste material;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the system for monitoring
    compliance with the discharge limits referred to in paragraph (f),
    including the sampling and analytical programs for determining
    whether discharges are within the specified limits;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a description of the organizational structure and
    chain of command for the proposed work or activity that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} explains the relationship between the
      organizational structure and chain of command, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} includes the name, position and contact
      information of the employee who is responsible for the management
      of the environmental protection plan;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures to be implemented to
    monitor compliance with the plan and to evaluate performance in
    relation to its objectives; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a description of the procedure to be followed if an
    archaeological site or a burial ground is discovered during the
    proposed work or activity.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contingency plan

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must develop a contingency plan that sets out the procedures,
  including emergency response procedures, and the practices, resources
  and monitoring measures that are necessary to effectively prepare for
  and mitigate the effects of any accidental event.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents and information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The contingency plan must include the following
  documents and information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the method to be used for
    classifying accidental events and a description of the emergency
    response procedures for each event;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the procedures for the internal
    and external reporting of accidental events;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the procedures for accessing
    safety-related and environmental information that is necessary to
    mitigate the effects of any accidental event;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the organizational structure,
    chain of command and resources for managing any accidental event,
    including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a list of key emergency response positions and a
      description of the roles, responsibilities and authorities
      associated with each of those positions, including a description
      of related tasks and checklists of actions that must be taken in
      the context of the contingency plan,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the available support craft and
      the contact information for its crew or a reference to the number
      or title of a document that provides that description and contact
      information,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description, or a reference to the number or
      title of a document that provides the description, of available
      emergency response equipment, including life-saving appliances,
      and the equipment's location, as well as the limits on its use and
      the mitigation measures to be taken in the event that it is not
      available,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a description, or a reference to a number or
      title of a document that provides the description, of all
      available medical equipment and the equipment's location,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a description of the communication system
      referred to in section 129 and the operating procedures for that
      system,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a description of all emergency response
      operations centres and their locations,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a description of any good or service that must
      be obtained on a contractual basis for each response measure, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the location and the contents
      of any temporary safe refuges or a reference to the number or
      title of a document that provides that description;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} details of all mutual aid agreements entered into
    with other operators;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of the procedures for coordinating
    and liaising with all relevant emergency response organizations;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the communication protocols with
    all relevant federal, provincial, territorial and municipal agencies
    and Indigenous governing bodies;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a copy of all personnel evacuation plans, including
    any evacuation plan for divers engaged in a dive; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the frequency with which emergency
    response drills and exercises are to be completed and their scope.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uncontrolled flow

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a drilling program or a production
  project, the contingency plan must also include a description of the
  source control and containment measures to be taken to stop
  uncontrolled flow from a well and to minimize the duration and
  environmental effects of any resulting spill, as well as the following
  documents and information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the source control and containment
    equipment to be used in the event of a loss of well control;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} details of the contractual arrangements for the
    source control and containment equipment, other than a relief well
    drilling installation, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name and contact information of the owner or
      owners of the equipment,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the arrangements for transport of the equipment
      to the location of the uncontrolled well, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the arrangements for the mode of deployment of
      the equipment at the location of the uncontrolled well;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the schedule and plan for the mobilization,
    deployment and operation of the source control and containment
    equipment, including measures to minimize deployment time that take
    required regulatory approvals into account;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} details regarding the accessibility of the source
    control and containment equipment and the documents and information
    referred to in paragraphs (a) to (c);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an explanation of the adequacy of each of the
    source control and containment measures; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of any support systems and equipment
    that are available, including vessels and remotely operated vehicles
    and any consumables that may be used, such as, in the case of a
    relief well, a spare wellhead, spare casing and spare bulk
    additives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spill-treating agent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a spill-treating agent is being considered for use
  as a spill response measure, the contingency plan must include the
  following additional documents and information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the chosen spill-treating agent and
    details of an assessment of its efficacy in treating the potential
    sources of pollutants, including the results of any tests conducted
    for the assessment and a description of those tests;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the results of an analysis that demonstrates that a
    net environmental benefit is likely to be achieved through the use
    of the spill-treating agent under certain circumstances;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the circumstances under which the
    spill-treating agent will be used and the estimated period within
    which its use will be effective;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the methods and protocols,
    including the amount and application rate, for safe, effective and
    efficient use of the spill-treating agent;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a list of the personnel roles, equipment and
    materials that an operator will have available for the purpose of
    using the spill-treating agent, including any to be provided through
    contractual arrangements, and a description of the requirements that
    must be met for those contracts to be activated; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a monitoring plan for the use of the spill-treating
    agent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assessment of efficacy

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The assessment of efficacy under paragraph (4)(a)
  must be carried out using oil obtained directly from the operations
  site where the spill-treating agent is being considered for use or, if
  oil is not available from that operations site, it must be carried out
  using an oil that most closely resembles the oil that is expected to
  be obtained from the operations site and must be repeated when oil
  becomes available from that operations site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}International standard or alternative

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The assessment, analysis, methods and protocols
  referred to in paragraphs (4)(a), (b) and (d) must be based, taking
  the local environment into account, on an international standard or an
  alternative recognized by the Board and the contingency plan must
  identify each of those standards or alternatives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Methods and protocols

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The methods and protocols referred to in paragraph
  (4)(d) and the monitoring plan referred to in paragraph (4)(f) must
  conform to industry standards and best practices for spill-treating
  agent use, taking the local environment into account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[source control and
  containment equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[source control and containment
  equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the capping stack, containment
  dome, any subsea and surface equipment, devices or vessels and any
  relief well drilling installations that are used to contain and
  control a spill source and to minimize the duration of a spill and its
  environmental effects until well control is regained.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spill-treating agent --- section 142.21
of Act

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** In determining for the
purpose of section 142.21 of the Act whether the use of a spill-treating
agent is likely to achieve a net environmental benefit, the Board must
take into account

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the assessment of the spill-treating agent's efficacy
  referred to in paragraph 11(4)(a);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the results of the analysis referred to in paragraph
  11(4)(b);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances referred to in paragraph 11(4)(c);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the methods and protocols referred to in paragraph
  11(4)(d); and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the monitoring plan referred to in paragraph
  11(4)(f).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Field data acquisition program

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
production project, an operator must develop a field data acquisition
program that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} provides for the collection of sufficient pool
  pressure measurements, drill cutting and fluid samples, cores and well
  logs, and the carrying out of sufficient formation flow tests,
  analyses and surveys, to enable a comprehensive assessment of the
  field, of the performance of development wells, of the pool depletion
  scheme and of any injection scheme; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} identifies the quantity of samples and cores, the
  evaluation data and any associated analyses, surveys and reports that
  are to be provided to the Board.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Flow system, calculation and allocation

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  application for an authorization is in respect of a production
  project, the operator must submit to the Board for its approval the
  flow system, the flow calculation procedure and the flow allocation
  procedure that will be used to conduct the measurements referred to in
  sections 74 to 78, as well as any alternate measurements referred to
  in subsection 74(2) that the operator proposes to conduct.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board must approve the flow system, the flow
  calculation procedure and the flow allocation procedure if the
  applicant demonstrates that the system and procedures facilitate
  accurate measurements and the allocation, on a pool or zone basis, of
  the production from and injection into individual wells.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decommissioning and abandonment plan

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must, in the case of a drilling program or production
  project, develop a decommissioning and abandonment plan that includes
  the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety and environmental
    protection measures to be implemented during the decommissioning and
    abandonment to comply with the requirements of these Regulations,
    the provisions of Part III of the Act and any federal or provincial
    legislation or international conventions or agreements relating to
    safety and the protection of the environment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the potential effects of the
    decommissioning and abandonment on the environment and on any future
    uses of the site where the program or project is carried out;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the methods for restoring the site after the
    decommissioning and abandonment; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the forecasted costs of the decommissioning and
    abandonment and the manner in which the operator will finance or pay
    for those costs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs and financing or payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator must submit to the Board an update on
  the forecasted costs of decommissioning and abandonment and the manner
  in which the operator will finance or pay for those costs

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whenever there is a significant change to that
    information, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} beginning no less than five years before the day on
    which the decommissioning and abandonment is forecasted to begin, at
    least once a year.

### [Requirements for Authorization]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1444926 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions --- paragraph 142(4)(c) of
Act

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply for the purposes of paragraph 142(4)(c) of the Act.

[[production facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[production facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the systems and
    equipment referred to in paragraph (a) of the definition
    [[production installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, other than a
    diving system, as well as any associated aircraft landing areas,
    storage areas or tanks and accommodations areas. ([installation de
    production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[production platform]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[production platform]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a production
    installation. ([plate-forme de production]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

### [Well Approvals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1444931 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Well operation

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), an
  operator that intends to conduct a well operation must obtain a well
  approval.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval not necessary

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A well approval is not necessary to conduct a wire
  line operation, slick line operation, coiled tubing operation or other
  similar operation through a tree located above sea level if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the operation does not alter the completion
    interval or is not expected to adversely affect the recovery of
    petroleum; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the equipment, operating procedures and
    qualifications of the persons carrying out the work are in
    compliance with the requirements of the authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in subsection (2).

  [[slick line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[slick line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a single steel cable that
      is used to run tools in a well. ([câble lisse]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[wire line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wire line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a line that contains a
      conductor wire and that is used to run survey instruments or other
      tools in a well. ([câble]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval application contents

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The application for a well approval must include the
  estimated cost breakdown of the well operation and the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the well approval is being sought to drill a
    well,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a comprehensive description of the drilling
      program, a geoscientific description of the reservoir targets and
      a description of any geohazard,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the digital data necessary to allow for an
      independent geohazard assessment,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the well data acquisition
      program referred to in section 18, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a description of the well verification scheme
      referred to in section 19;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the well approval is being sought to perform a
    workover on, to re-enter, to complete or to recomplete a well or to
    suspend or abandon a well or a part of one, a description of the
    well or part, a description of the proposed work or activity and the
    rationale for carrying it out and barrier envelope diagrams that
    demonstrate that two barrier envelopes will be in place throughout
    the operation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the well approval is being sought to complete a
    well, information that demonstrates that section 71 will be complied
    with;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the well approval is being sought to suspend a
    well or a part of one, an indication of the period within which the
    suspended well or part will be abandoned or completed; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the well approval is being sought to suspend or
    abandon a well or a part of one, the methods for verifying the
    effectiveness of the isolation of pools and zones that is required
    under subparagraph 90(1)(b)(i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Well approval granted by the Board

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Board must grant the well approval if the
  operator demonstrates that the well operation will be conducted
  safely, without waste or pollution and in compliance with these
  Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Well data acquisition program

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a drilling
program, an operator must develop a well data acquisition program that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} provides for the collection of sufficient pressure
  measurements, drill cutting and fluid samples, conventional cores,
  sidewall cores and well logs, and the carrying out of sufficient
  formation flow tests, analyses and surveys, to enable a comprehensive
  geophysical, geological and reservoir evaluation to be made; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} identifies the quantity of samples and cores, the
  evaluation data and any associated analyses, surveys and reports that
  are to be provided to the Board.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Well verification scheme

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must establish a well verification scheme based on criteria
  that the operator establishes to ensure that the design of any well is
  in accordance with industry standards and best practices so that the
  well's integrity is maintained throughout its life cycle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Well ranking

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the operator must
  rank a well according to its level of risk and ensure that the well
  ranking is confirmed by an independent person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification requirements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The verification scheme must set out the verification
  requirements that are applicable to the design of a well according to
  the well's ranking and to any changes made to the design during the
  well's construction or operation that would affect any previously
  undertaken verification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification by independent person

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The operator must ensure that the required
  verifications are carried out by an independent person that was not
  involved in the original design.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of well approval

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Board may suspend a well approval if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the operator conducts the well operation other than
    as described in the application for the well approval;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the physical and environmental conditions
    encountered in the area of the work or activity for which the well
    approval was granted are more severe than those on the basis of
    which the manufacturer of any equipment used in the well operation
    established the equipment's operating limits; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the operator uses a flow system, flow calculation
    procedure or flow allocation procedure that has not been approved
    under subsection 14(2), conducts a formation flow test that has not
    been approved under subsection 63(5) or engages in commingled
    production that has not been approved under subsection 80(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors for suspension

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In deciding whether to suspend a well approval, the
  Board must consider

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the effects or potential effects of the applicable
    situation referred to in subsection (1) on safety, the environment
    and the conservation of petroleum resources; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator's history of non-compliance with the
    requirements of these Regulations, the provisions of Part III of the
    Act or any requirements that are established by the Board under that
    Part with respect to well operations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of well approval

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** The Board must revoke a
well approval if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the operator fails to remedy the situation that
  caused the suspension of the well approval as soon as the
  circumstances permit within 60 days after the date of that suspension
  unless, on written request by the operator, the Board grants the
  operator an extension of time to remedy the situation; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator continues to operate the well despite
  the suspension of the well approval.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or abandonment of well

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** If a well approval is
revoked, the operator must ensure that the well is suspended or
abandoned in accordance with Part 8.

### [Development Plan]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1444985 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Well approval --- subsection 143(1) of
Act

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 143(1) of the Act, a well approval relating to a production
project is prescribed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Concept safety analysis

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  approvals referred to in subsection 143(4) of the Act are subject to
  the operator's submission of a concept safety analysis to the Chief
  Safety Officer at the time the operator submits the application and
  proposed development plan to the Board under subsection 143(2) of the
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The concept safety analysis must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be based on the development concept chosen by the
    operator as a general approach and described in Part I of the
    development plan;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} take into account all works and activities
    associated with each phase in the life cycle of the development;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} determine target levels of safety that are to be
    achieved to ensure safety and the protection of the environment for
    all works and activities within each phase of the life cycle of an
    installation, including its systems and equipment, from the
    installation's design up to and including its decommissioning and
    abandonment;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} identify all hazards having the potential to cause
    a major accidental event;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} include a systematic assessment of the unmitigated
    risks associated with each of the identified hazards, including the
    likelihood of a major accidental event occurring and the
    consequences that would result;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} identify the control measures that are to be
    implemented to reduce the risks associated with the identified
    hazards to a level that is as low as reasonably practicable;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} identify the effects of any additional risks that
    may result from the implementation of the identified control
    measures; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} identify all assumptions on which any aspect of the
    concept safety analysis is based.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantitative and qualitative risk
  assessments

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The target levels of safety must be based on risk
  assessments that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} quantitative, if it can be demonstrated that input
    data are available in the quantity and quality necessary to
    demonstrate the reliability of the results; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} qualitative, if the criteria in paragraph (a) are
    not met or if a quantitative assessment would otherwise be
    inappropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of risk assessment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The operator must include in the risk assessment a
  description of the circumstances that will necessitate an update of
  the risk assessment, including changes in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the physical and environmental conditions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operating conditions and the limits taken into
    account in the design assumptions; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the operating procedures.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of risk assessment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The operator must update the risk assessment as often
  as necessary and at least once every five years throughout the life
  cycle of the development to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} account for the circumstances described in
    subsection (4); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure the ongoing suitability of the control
    measures to maintain risks at a level as low as reasonably
    practicable.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resource management plan --- paragraph
143(3)(b) of Act

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 143(3)(b) of the Act, Part II of the
  development plan must contain a resource management plan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of resource management plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The resource management plan must include a
  description and analysis of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the geological setting and features of the field
    and of each pool or petroleum-bearing reservoir;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the petrophysical data and analytical procedures
    for each pool;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the reservoir engineering data for each pool;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} estimates of in-place resources and recoverable
    reserves for each pool, fault block and reservoir subdivision;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the proposed reservoir exploitation scheme;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} potential developments and the reasons why they are
    not included in the proposed development of the field or pool;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any past drilling in the area related to the
    proposed development of the field or pool as well as the proposed
    drilling program and typical completion designs for the development
    wells;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the production and export systems related to the
    proposed development of the field or pool;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the expected overall operating efficiency and
    reliability of the proposed development of the field or pool; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} past expenditures and predicted capital and
    operating cost data, with sufficient detail to permit an economic
    analysis of the proposed development of the field or pool.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Organizational structure

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The resource management plan must also contain a
  description of the operator's organizational structure as it relates
  to the implementation of the plan.

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Certificate of Fitness]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1445031 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1445032 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed installations --- section
143.2 of Act

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
section 143.2 of the Act, a production installation, drilling
installation, accommodations installation and diving installation are
prescribed installations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[installation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an installation referred to
in section 26.

### [Requirements for Certification]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1445037 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of certificate --- requirements
and conditions

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  a certifying authority issues a certificate of fitness in respect of
  an installation,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person that applies for the certificate must

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} provide the certifying authority with all the
